{D157}]@##/_/rgya gar skad du/_Ar+ya dru ma ki nan ra rA dza pa ri prIts+tshA nA ma ma hA yA na sU tra/_bod skad du/_'phags pa mi[(] [,)]'am ci'i rgyal po sdong pos zhus pa zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo/_bam po dang po/_sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad 
[\[254a.2\]]la phyag 'tshal lo/_/'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/_bcom ldan 'das rgyal po'i khab na ri bya rgod kyi phung po la dge slong drug khri nyis stong gi dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang /_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po phal cher phyogs bcu'i 
[\[254a.3\]]'jig rten gyi khams nas 'dus pa bdun khri nyis stong thams cad kyang mngon par shes pas mngon par shes pa/_gzungs rab tu thob pa/_spobs pa chags pa med pa/_ting nge 'dzin rab tu thob pa/_dran pa dang /_blo gros dang /_rtogs pa dang /_mos pa dang /_ngo tsha shes pa 
[\[254a.4\]]dang /_khrel yod pa phun sum tshogs pa/_bsam pa rdo rje ltar brtan zhing mi phyed pa/_sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad la sbyor ba phun sum tshogs pa/_lhag pa'i bsam pa khyad par du 'phags pa la zhugs pa/_bdag nyid byang chub kyi sems mi 'dor ba/_sems 
[\[254a.5\]]can gzhan byang chub kyi sems yang dag par 'dzin du 'jug pa/_sbyin pa dang /_dul ba dang /_sdom pa dang /_des pa dang /_bzod pa dang /_sdug pa'i dngos po thams cad yongs su gtong ba dang /_yongs su bsngo ba'i cho ga shes pa/_lus dang /_ngag dang /_sems 
[\[254a.6\]]dang /_tshul khrims kyi rgyan gyis legs par brgyan pa/_sems can thams cad la sems snyoms pa dang /_bzod pa dang /_des pa'i stobs kyi go cha shin tu bgos pa/_bskal pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong phrag grangs med par brtson 'grus shin tu brtsams pa/_bsam 
[\[254a.7\]]gtan dang /_rnam par thar pa dang /_ting nge 'dzin dang /_snyoms par 'jug pa dang /_mngon par shes pa'i ye shes kyis rnam par rol pa/_tshig thams cad rab tu dbye ba la spobs pa chags pa med pa'i shes rab dang /_ye shes la mkhas pa/_ri rab kyi ri ltar 
[\[254b\]]
[\[254b.1\]]sems g.yo ba med pa/_sa dang /_chu dang /_me dang /_rlung dang /_ri ltar rjes su chags pa dang /_khong khro ba dang bral ba byams pa chen po la gnas te 'od zer thogs pa med pa/_snying rje chen po la gnas te sems can thams cad kyi bya ba gcig la brtson pa/_dga' 
[\[254b.2\]]ba chen po la gnas te chos la dga' zhing mgu ba dang ldan pa/_btang snyoms chen po la gnas par brtson pa/_gnyis su med pa'i ye shes rab tu thob pa/_'jig rten gyi chos brgyad po rnyed pa dang /_ma rnyed pa dang /_grags pa dang /_ma grags pa dang /_
[\[254b.3\]]bstod pa dang /_smad pa dang /_bde ba dang /_sdug bsngal las rab tu 'das pa/_pha rol rgol ba ngan pa'i tshogs can gyi tshogs thams cad kun du rab tu 'joms pa/_bdud dang phyir rgol ba bcom pa/_u dum bA ra'i me tog mthong ba ltar shin tu 'byung bar dka' 
[\[254b.4\]]ba/_gsol ba ma btab par sems can thams cad la dge ba'i bshes gnyen byed pa/_rab tu yongs su mya ngan las 'da' ba'i bshes gnyen byed pa/_seng ge'i sgra chen po sgrogs pa/_sangs rgyas kyi chos zab mo dang mi 'jigs pa sgrogs pa/_de bzhin gshegs 
[\[254b.5\]]pa'i phyag rgyas btab pa/_mi 'khrugs par phyad par lung ston pa/_ji ltar smras pa/_de bzhin byed pa/_de kho na'i chos kyi tshul la brtson pa 'od kyis nyi ma dang zla ba mog mog por byed pa/_phyogs bcur sgra bsgrags pa/_sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad la sems pa/_
[\[254b.6\]]chos dkon mchog gi mdzod yang dag par 'dzin pa/_dkon mchog gsum gyi rigs mi gcod par yid la byed pa/_sangs rgyas kyi zhing mtha' yas pa 'da' ba la mkhas pa/_shin tu rnam par phye ba rnam par 'byed pa gnas pa'i blo dang ldan pa mthong ba dang /_thos 
[\[254b.7\]]pa don yod pas rnam par gnon pa/_rtag par rgyun du sems can yongs su smin par byed pa la brtson pa/_shes rab dang thabs la mkhas pa'i mchog thob pa/_sems can thams cad kyi dbang po mchog dang mchog ma yin pa shes pas ci rigs par chos ston pa/_
[\[255a\]]
[\[255a.1\]]sems can thams cad kyi spyod pa la lta ba'i cho ga la mkhas pa/_sems can thams cad kyi 'jug pa dang btags pa la bde blag tu mkhas pa/_mi 'jigs pa chen pos 'jigs pa med pa/_rga ba dang /_na ba dang /_'chi ba dang /_nyon mongs 
[\[255a.2\]]pa rab tu zhi bar byed pa/_sangs rgyas bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong dpag tu med pa la dge ba'i rtsa ba bskrun pa/_mtshan dang dpe byad bzang po yongs su rdzogs pa'i bsod nams dang /_ye shes kyi tshogs bsags pa/_stong pa nyid dang /_mtshan ma med pa dang /_
[\[255a.3\]]smon pa med pa'i chos thams cad la mos pa la/_smon pa med pa sems can dang /_chos thams cad sgyu ma dang /_smig rgyu dang /_rmi lam dang /_chu zla dang /_mig yor dang /_brag ca dang /_nam mkha' dang mtshungs par mos pa/_sgra skad thams cad nges pa'i 
[\[255a.4\]]tshig gis ston pa'i ye shes la mkhas pa/_chos dang /_don dang nges pa'i tshig dang /_spobs pa so so yang dag par rig pa thob pa/_'jig rten las 'das pa'i shes rab kyi dbang po mngon sum du gyur pa/_shugs chen po 'dzin pa'i stobs bcu la nye bar gyur pa/_
[\[255a.5\]]sha'i mig dang /_lha'i mig dang /_shes rab kyi mig dang /_chos kyi mig dang /_sangs rgyas kyi spyan snang ba thob pa/_lam gyi yan lag gi tshogs shin tu bsags pa'i lam thams cad la 'jug pa'i ye shes la mkhas pa/_byang chub sems dpa'i sde snod snang ba la 
[\[255a.6\]]'jug pa'i cho ga shes pa/_phyir mi ldog pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba/_mtshan nyid kyi phyag rgya'i ting nge 'dzin rab tu thob pa/_rdo rje dkyil 'khor gyi ting nge 'dzin la 'jug pa'i ye shes la mkhas pa/_chos thams cad la dbang byed pa'i ting nge 'dzin mngon du gyur pa/_chos 
[\[255a.7\]]dkon mchog gi sgron ma 'dzin pa/_sems can thams cad kyi sems kyi rgyud la rnam par lta ba/_sems can thams cad kyi sems kyi bsam pa la rnam par lta bar mkhas pa/_sems can thams cad la sangs rgyas kyi ye shes kyi bya bas so sor nye bar gnas pa rnam 
[\[255b\]]
[\[255b.1\]]pa'i mchog thams cad dang ldan pa'i yon tan rin po che thams cad kyis brgyan pa/_bskal pa thams cad du bsngags pa yongs su brjod kyang mthar mi phyin pa sha stag la 'di lta ste/_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po dga' byed dang /_dga' 'byung dang /_
[\[255b.2\]]lag na rin po che dang /_rin chen me tog dang /_rin po che'i tog dang /_mthong dga' dang /_mig gis mthong dga' dang /_mdzes pa'i blo gros dang /_dga' zhing mchog tu dga' byed dang /_sa 'dzin dang /_spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug dang /_mthu chen thob dang /_
[\[255b.3\]]dpal chen dang /_bdud thul dang /_me tog man dA ra ba'i dri dang /_rlung lha dang /_bden dga' dang /_mnyam lta dang /_ri rab rtse 'dzin dang /_ri rab brtsegs dang /_ri rab mchog dang /_dra ba can gyi 'od dang /_rgyal ba'i blo gros dang /_skye bar tshim byed 
[\[255b.4\]]dang /_'gro ba 'dzin dang /_lha'i rgyal pos mchod dang /_mchog gi blo gros dang /_thogs med sems dang /_skya ring blo gros dang /_mi skyob pa dang /_dpag med rin chen mdzod dang /_'od kyi tog dang /_'od dpal dang /_sgron ma'i rgyal po dang /_byang chub blo gros dang /_
[\[255b.5\]]'od kyis yang dag brgyan dang /_sna tshogs me tog dang /_lha'i rgyal po dang /_lha'i tshul gyis lta ba dang /_dpung bzang dang /_legs par bsam pa sems pa dang /_'khor lo rab gnas rkang pa dang /_mi g.yo rkang pas rnam par gnon dang /_rdo rje'i rkang pas rnam par 
[\[255b.6\]]gnon dang /_rkang pas 'jig rten gsum rnam par gnon dang /_dga' spobs dang /_spobs myur dang /_spobs pa mi 'chad pa dang /_spobs pa byin gyis brlabs dang /_'jam sgra dang /_sems can thams cad tshim byed dang /_tshangs dbyangs dang /_bzang skyong

[\[256a.1\]]dang thabs cig tu bzhugs so/_/gang stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di'i brgya byin dang /_tshangs pa dang /_'jig rten skyong ba dang /_de las gzhan pa lha dang /_klu dang /_gnod sbyin dang /_dri za dang /_lha ma yin dang /_nam mkha'

[\[256a.5\]]'jig rten don mdzad blo gros ldan/_/snang ba brnyes shing nyon mongs dri bsal ba/_/'jig rten snang mdzad khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/sbyin la dgyes shing stobs bcu mchog mnga' ba/_/dul ba 'dul ba gdul ba'i pha rol phyin/_/bcom ldan lha dang mi dang klus mchod pa/_/gdul 
[\[256a.6\]]ba'i mthar phyin khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/mtshan mchog 'gro ba mang po'i don mdzad pa/_/'dom gang 'od ni gser gyi kha dog lta/_/dge ba'i mtshan gyi dam pa'i gzugs mnga' ba/_/srid gsum mtshungs med khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/pha rol phyin pas gzhan gyi

[\[256b.1\]]gnas/_/lha yi lha mchog dri med dri ma bral/_/pha rol tshogs 'joms khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/bde gshegs rin chen mchog tu dga' mdzad pa/_/dgon par rtag gnas rtag tu dgon par dgyes/_/rdul skyon gti mug rnyog pa mi mnga' ba/_/nam mkha' mnyam thugs 
[\[256b.2\]]khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/chu bo bzhi rgal bden bzhi bka' mchid gsung /_/mig sbyin bdud rtsi chos kyi bka' mchid can/_/'gro ba'i don mdzad 'jig rten gsum rgyu ba/_/'khor lo zhabs mnga' khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/lha dang mis mchod pa yi mthu bo che/_/mgon

[\[256b.7\]]skye gang du'ang skye ma lags/_/skye dang 'jig dang gang du'ang mi bas pa/_/chos kyi gnas nyid ji lta'i gnas la gnas/_/gnas la mi gnas rgyal la phyag 'tshal lo/_/de bzhin kho na de bzhin yang dag gsung /_/rtag tu mi g.yo de bzhin nyid la bzhugs/_/ri rab rgyal mtshan 
[\[257a\]]
[\[257a.1\]]rdo rje 'dra ba'i sku/_/mi g.yo ri rab mtshungs la phyag 'tshal lo/_/sku ni dben zhing mnyam thugs blo ldan pa/_/grags pa mtha' yas 'jig rten gsum grags pa/_/skyes bu mchog la zhu ba zhus pa rnams/_/'jig rten zla bas myur du skabs dbyer 
[\[257a.2\]]gsol/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po lha'i cod pan gyis tshul dang 'thun pa'i tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gis bcom ldan 'das la mngon par bstod nas 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das gal te bdag gis zhus pa lung 
[\[257a.3\]]bstan pa'i slad du/_de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyis skabs dbye bar gnang na bcom ldan 'das la bdag phyogs 'ga' zhig zhu'o/_/de skad ces gsol pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' 
[\[257a.4\]]sems dpa' chen po lha'i cod pan la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/rigs kyi bu khyod de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas la ci dang ci 'dod pa dris shig dang /_ngas dri ba dris pa de dang de'i lung bstan pas khyod kyi sems 
[\[257a.5\]]rangs par bya'o/_/de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po lha'i cod pan gyis bcom ldan 'das la legs so zhes gsol te/_bcom ldan 'das kyi ltar nyan cing bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 
[\[257a.6\]]'das ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' spobs pa sna tshogs kyi rgyan dang ldan pa rnams lags/_ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' chos zab mo rnam par gdon mi za ba la 'jug pa shes pa la mkhas pa rnams lags/_ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems can thams

[\[257b.5\]]byang chub sems dpa' nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas thams cad dang /_sems can thams cad kyi spyod lam yang ston la mtshungs par spyod par yang rnam par mi rtog pa rnams lags/_ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' 'khor ba la yang lta la mya ngan las 'das pa 
[\[257b.6\]]la yang mi gnas pa rnams lags/_ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' khams sna tshogs la yang rnam par lta la chos kyi dbyings las kyang mi g.yo ba rnams lags/_ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' don yang mi 'dor la gnod par yang kun du gda' ba rnams lags/_

[\[258a\]]
[\[258a.1\]]rgyas kyi chos thams cad mngon sum du 'gyur ba rnams lags/_ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' chos rnam par gdon mi za ba rnams la rnam par phye ba shin tu rnam par 'byed pa rnams lags/_ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' 'jig rten gyi bya ba dang 
[\[258a.2\]]'thun par zhugs kyang nyon mongs pa thams cad kyis mi gos pa rnams lags/_ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' gzhan dag gis bltar mi mthong ba'i chos kyi dbang phyug rnams lags/_ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs pa mthong ba dang mi 'bral ba 
[\[258a.3\]]rnams lags/_ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' shin tu dul zhing mi 'khrugs pa'i chos can rnams lags/_ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' chos rnams blangs pa ma mchis par slob dpon rnams lags/_ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems can thams cad 
[\[258a.4\]]kyi ston par gyur pa rnams lags/_ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas thams cad kyi chos kyi 'khor lo rjes su bskor ba rnams lags/_ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad dbang bskur ba thob pa rnams lags/_de skad

[\[258a.6\]]la snying brtse ba dang /_skye bo phal po che'i don dang /_lha dang mi rnams la phan pa dang /_bde ba'i phyir zhugs pa dang /_rigs kyi bu khyod da ltar gyi dang /_ma 'ongs pa'i sems can rnams rjes su gzung ba dang /_theg pa chen po 'di yun ring du gnas par bya ba'i phyir/_rigs 
[\[258a.7\]]kyi bu khyod de bzhin gshegs pa la gang don 'di yongs su 'dri bar sems pa ni legs so/_/rigs kyi bu de'i phyir shin tu legs par nyon la yid la zung shig dngangs khyod la bshad do/_/bcom ldan 'das legs so zhes gsol nas/_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa'

[\[258b.1\]]chen po lha'i cod pan bcom ldan 'das kyi ltar nyan pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyis de la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/rigs kyi bu byang chub sems dpa' chos bzhi dang ldan na spobs pa sna tshogs kyi rgyan dang ldan pa rnams yin no/_/bzhi gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_
[\[258b.2\]]sems can thams cad la 'od zer thogs pa med pa rnams rin po che dang sdug pa'i dngos po thams cad yongs su gtong zhing yid 'gyod pa med pa rnams yin/_chos smra ba gzhan dag gis bshad pa mi zlog cing rjes su yi rang ste dga' ba dang /_mgu ba dang /_dang 
[\[258b.3\]]ba dang mchog tu dga' ba thob nas legs so zhes bya ba sbyin pa dang /_chos mnyan pa'am/_chos bgro ba'am/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i mchod rten gyi phyir ram/_mtshan mo 'dug par bskul na mchog tu dga' ba dang ldan pa dang /_byang chub kyi sems kyi lhag pa'i 
[\[258b.4\]]bsam pa dang /_zang zing med pa'i sems kyi chos kyi sbyin pa sbyin te/_rigs kyi bu byang chub sems dpa' chos 'di bzhi dang ldan na spobs pa sna tshogs rgyan dang ldan pa rnams yin no/_/rigs kyi bu byang chub sems dpa' chos bzhi dang ldan na chos zab mo rnam par gdon mi 
[\[258b.5\]]za ba la 'jug pa shes pa la mkhas pa rnams yin no/_/bzhi gang zhe na/_rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba dang rjes su 'thun pa dang /_bdag gi rang bzhin ltar sems can thams cad kyi rang bzhin rtogs pa dang /_'du byed thams cad gar yang mi 'gro/_gang nas kyang ma 'ongs

[\[268b.2\]]pa des thams cad bzod de/_de stong par yang bzod de gang zag tu yang bzod do/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_gang zag las stong pa gzhan ma yin te/_gang zag nyid stong pa'o/_/de mtshan mar yang bzod de mtshan ma med par bzod pa'o/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_mtshan ma'i ngo bo nyid 
[\[268b.3\]]mtshan ma med pa'o/_/de smon par yang bzod/_de smon pa med pa yang bzod do/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_smon lam gyi ngo bo nyid kyi mtshan nyid smon pa med pa'o/_/de sems can thams cad rang bzhin gyis yongs su mya ngan las 'das par yang bzod de sems can rnams 'chi 'pho ba 
[\[268b.4\]]dang skye bar yang bzod do/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'chi 'pho dang skye ba ni sgyu ma'i ngo bo nyid do/_/rigs kyi bu de ltar mi skye ba'i chos la bzod pa rab tu thob pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ni 'gag pa med pa ste/_chos thams cad dang 'gal ba med pa yin no/_/bdag bzod pa'i rjes su 
[\[268b.5\]]'gro yang sems can thams cad kyi rjes su 'gro bas kyang 'gro/_bdag bzod pa rjes su 'gro yang chos thams cad kyi rjes su 'gro bas kyang 'gro ste/_'ong ba yang med/_'gro ba yang med do/_/chos thams cad chos kyi dbyings la gnas par yang yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin 
[\[268b.6\]]du rab tu shes so/_/ji ltar chos kyi dbyings la gnas pa de ltar sems can thams cad gnas te/_gang gi gnas de 'thob pa'i gnas med pa'i tshul gyis rjes su thob pa de sgra'i brda'i tha snyad kyi sgra nye bar bzung nas/_mi skye ba'i chos la bzod pa dang ldan pa zhes bya'o/_/mi 
[\[268b.7\]]skye ba'i chos la bzod pa ni gang gis kyang brjod pa'am/_bsnyad pa'am/_bstan par mi nus so/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_don de ni brjod du med pa/_sgra med pa/_bshad du med pa yin yang brjod du med pa'i don brjod pas bstan pa 'di ni rigs kyi bu de bzhin

[\[269a.1\]]gshegs pa'i che ba nyid do/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' lha'i cod pan gyis 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das kye ma mi'am ci'i rgyal po sdong po 'di ji tsam du chos zab mo la bzod pa ston par spobs pa dang /_'di lta bur

[\[269a.3\]]bskrun na des 'di spobs pa 'di lta bu grub par gyur/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_rigs kyi bu gang gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams kyi skar ma'i gzugs ji snyed pa bgrang ba'i tshul gyis tshad gzung bar nus sam/_de bzhin du mi'am ci'i rgyal po sdong po 
[\[269a.4\]]'dis yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas ji snyed cig la dge ba'i rtsa ba bskrun pa dang /_bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub btsal ba'i phyir de bzhin gshegs pa de dag gi gnan tshangs par spyod pa spyad pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i

[\[269b.1\]]pa dang /_chos mnyan pas mi ngoms pa dang /_dam pa'i chos yongs su gzung bas mi ngoms pa dang /_chos rgya cher rab tu bstan pas mi ngoms pa dang /_sems can yongs su smin par bya bas mi ngoms pa dang /_dge ba'i rtsa ba yang dag par bsgrub pas mi ngoms pa dang /_dgon

[\[287a.1\]]sangs rgyas kyi mthus de dag thams cad 'khor gyi khyams der bcom ldan 'das kyi dbu'i drang thad kyi steng gi bar snang la dpag tshad stong tsam na/_rin po che'i gdugs gcig tu gnas par gyur to/_/de nas mi'am ci'i rgyal po sdong po'i bu brgyad stong pos bcom ldan 
[\[287a.2\]]'das kyi cho 'phrul chen po 'di mthong nas tshim zhing mgu la yi rangs shing rab tu dga' ste/_dga' ba dang yid bde ba skyes nas/_lhag pa'i bsam pas bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed do/_/de dag gis byang chub tu sems bskyed nas/_bcom ldan 'das la 
[\[287a.3\]]'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das gang thos pas bdag cag byang chub kyi phyogs kyi chos yang dag par sgrub par 'gyur ba byang chub tu yongs su smin par bgyid pa'i chos de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyis bdag cag la bshad du gsol/_
[\[287a.4\]]de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis mi'am ci'i rgyal po sdong po'i bu de dag gi lhag pa'i bsam pa mkhyen nas ta la bdun srid kyi steng gi bar snang la skyil mo krung bcas te bzhugs nas sku las 'di lta bu'i 'od phyung ste/_'od des stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di snang ba 
[\[287a.5\]]chen pos khyab par gyur to/_/gang mi'am ci dang /_dri za dang /_lto 'phye de dag dang /_'dod pa na spyod pa'i lha'i bu de dag gi rol mo sil snyan rnam pa sna tshogs de dag thams cad ma brdungs ma dkrol bar mdzes pa dang ldan par 'khrol lo/_/ri'i rgyal po spos kyi ngad ldang na dri'i shing dang me 
[\[287a.6\]]tog gi shing dang 'bras bu'i shing ji snyed pa de dag las kyang rol mo sil snyan gyi sgra byung ngo /_/bcom ldan 'das kyi spu'i khung bu thams cad nas kyang 'od zer bye ba khrag khrig brgyad stong byung ngo /_/'od zer thams cad kyi rtse mo las kyang pad ma tshad shing rta'i 'khor lo tsam dag byung ngo /_/pad ma'i 
[\[287a.7\]]snying po thams cad la yang byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs skyes bu chen po mtshan sum cu rtsa gnyis kyis kun tu brgyan pa'i lus dag gnas par kun tu snang ngo /_/sangs rgyas kyi mthus rol mo sil snyan de dag las ni yongs su 'dri ba'i tshigs su bcad pa byung /_byang chub sems dpa' 
[\[287b\]]
[\[287b.1\]]de dag thams cad ni sgra dbyangs dang skad gcig gis tshigs su bcad nas nges par lan 'debs so/_/de la sil snyan las tshigs su bcad pa/_bla na med pa'i byang chub sems/_/ji lta bur ni skyed pa dang /_/byang chub snying por 'dug bar du/_/sems ni brjed pa med par 'gyur/_/
[\[287b.2\]]byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_sems can kun gyi ched kyi phyir/_/lhag pa'i bsam pa phun sum tshogs/_/snying rje che dang ldan pa ni/_/byang chub sems brjed mi 'gyur ro/_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_de dag bsam pa ji lta bu/_/rab tu 'byor pa 
[\[287b.3\]]ci 'dra zhig_/de dag snying rje chen po la/_/nges par sems pa ji ltar skye/_/byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_g.yo med lhag pa'i bsam pa ste/_/de dag sbyor ba bcos ma min/_/mya ngan 'da' sgor 'jog pa ni/_/de dag snying rje po sdong de 'dra'o/_/sil snyan 
[\[287b.4\]]rnams kyis smras pa/_de dag gtong ba ji lta bu/_/ji ltar byin nas 'gyod med cing /_/rnam smin don du mi gnyer bar/_/byang chub tu ni sngo bar byed/_/byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_dngos po kun la mi lta ba/_/des ni byin nas 'gyod pa med/_/byang 
[\[287b.5\]]chub lam du 'jog byed pa/_/de phyir rnam smin re ba med/_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_ji ltar tshul khrims ldan gyur kyang /_/tshul khrims kyis ni rlom sems med/_/theg chen bla na med pa la/_/ngang tshul ngan can smin par byed/_/byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs 
[\[287b.6\]]rnams kyis smras pa/_byang chub sems kyi tshul khrims ldan/_/stong pa nyid kyis rlom sems med/_/byams pa chen po skyed pa ni/_/ngang tshul ngan can yongs smin byed/_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_ji ltar na ni sems can gyi/_/spyos shing bsdigs pa bzod byed cing /_/
[\[287b.7\]]sems kyis gnod pa mi byed la/_/mchog tu dga' ba skye bar 'gyur/_/byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_bdag ni sems can thams cad kyi/_/nyon mongs gso ba'i sman pa snyam/_/de dag ngan par smras pa yi/_/tshig thos zhe sdang mi 'gyur ro/_/sil 
[\[288a\]]
[\[288a.1\]]snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_de dag brtson 'grus ji lta bu/_/pha rol gnon pa'ang ci 'dra zhig_/ji ltar zhum pa med sems kyis/_/byang chub spyad pa spyod par 'gyur/_/byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_chos dang sems can bsrung ba ni/_/
[\[288a.2\]]pha rol gnon dang brtson 'grus so/_/dge ba'i rtsa bas brtan pa ni/_/des na sems ni zhum mi 'gyur/_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_de dag ji ltar dran dang ldan/_/blo gros rtogs par khong du chud/_/ji ltar bsam gtan bsam gtan byed/_/de la chags pa med par 'gyur/_/
[\[288a.3\]]byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_g.yeng ba med pa dran dang ldan/_/tshul 'chos med pa blo gros ldan/_/thabs kyis bsam gtan bsam gtan byed/_/des na de la chags mi 'gyur/_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_ji ltar shes rab ldan pa dang /_/yang 
[\[288a.4\]]dag lta ba drang po dang /_/chos rnams rnam par dbye ba la/_/mkhas shing 'jigs pa med par 'gyur/_/byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_thos pas shes rab kun tu 'phel/_/rten pas lta ba drang por 'gyur/_/chos kyi sbyin pas nges 'gyur te/_/tshul 
[\[288a.5\]]bzhin gyis ni don rtogs 'gyur/_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_ji ltar thos pa btsal bas na/_/de dag thos pa mang du 'gyur/_/gang gis che ba'i bdag 'gyur bar/_/thos pa'i chos rnams ston par 'gyur/_/byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_
[\[288a.6\]]gus par byas te thos tshol zhing /_/sgrub pa'i thos pa mang du 'gyur/_/zang zing med par chos sbyin pa/_/des ni che ba'i bdag nyid 'gyur/_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_de dag byams pa ji lta bu/_/de dag snying rje ci 'dra zhig_/de dag dga' ba ji lta bu/_/btang snyoms 
[\[288a.7\]]kyang ni ci 'dra zhig_/byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_sems dang btang snyoms byams pa ste/_/sems can 'grol ba'i snying rje'o/_/sangs rgyas chos dga' dga' ba ste/_/gnyis spangs pa ni btang snyoms so/_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_
[\[288b\]]
[\[288b.1\]]ji ltar sangs rgyas mthong bar 'gyur/_/sems kyang ji ltar mthong bar 'gyur/_/chos thos nas ni ji lta bur/_/de dag the tshom skye mi 'gyur/_/byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_sangs rgyas rjes su dran bsgoms pa/_/des ni 'dren pa mthong bar 'gyur/_/
[\[288b.2\]]the tshom med cing bsam dag pas/_/chos thos nas ni som nyi med/_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_bsod nams tshogs ni ji lta bu/_/ye shes tshogs kyang ci 'dra zhig_/de dag zhi gnas lhag mthong la/_/brtson pa yang ni ji lta bu/_/byang chub sems dpa'i 
[\[288b.3\]]gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_ngoms mi shes pa bsod nams tshogs/_/'dri ba rnams ni ye shes tshogs/_/zhi ba'i sems ni zhi gnas te/_/chos shes pa ni lhag mthong ngo /_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_de dag gi ni spyod yul gang /_/de dag gnas kyang ji lta bu/_/
[\[288b.4\]]de dag gnas pa ci 'dra zhig_/de dag sbyor ba'ang ji lta bu/_/byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_de dag spyod yul stong pa nyid/_/gnas ni pha rol phyin pa'i lam/_/gnas pa bsam gtan bzhi rnams te/_/sems can rab grol sbyor ba'o/_/sil 
[\[288b.5\]]snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_de dag bdud kyi las gang yin/_/sangs rgyas phrin las ji lta bu/_/ci 'dra ba yi las byas na/_/byang chub la ni gzhol bar 'gyur/_/byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_theg pa chung ngu bdud kyi las/_/sangs rgyas phrin 
[\[288b.6\]]las theg pa che/_/sdig pa thams cad spong ba ni/_/byang chub gzhol bar 'gyur ba'o/_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_de dag grogs bsten ji lta bu/_/sdig pa'i grogs po ci 'dra zhig_/log par lta la mi gnas shing /_/ji ltar yang dag song bar 'gyur/_/byang 
[\[288b.7\]]chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_gang zhig byang chub lam ston pa/_/rtag tu bde sbyin grogs po'o/_/byang chub sems las gang zlog pa/_/de dag sdig pa'i grogs por shes/_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_/lam du 'jug par 'gyur ba yi/_/
[\[289a\]]
[\[289a.1\]]lta ba drang po ji lta bu/_/sbyor ba rnam par shes nas su/_/ji ltar lta de 'jig mi byed/_/byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_las dang bya bar 'jug pa ni/_/de dag drang por lta ba yin/_/log par sbyor ba rnam shes nas/_/lta ba de dag 
[\[289a.2\]]'jig mi byed/_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_ji ltar dam pa'i chos bsrung zhing /_/sems can yongs su smin par byed/_/ji lta bu yi thabs kyis ni/_/de dag byang chub bsgrub par 'gyur/_/byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_brtson 'grus kyis 
[\[289a.3\]]ni chos bsrung ste/_/bsdu bas yongs su smin par byed/_/mtha' gnyis rnam par spangs pas ni/_/byang chub sgrub par byed pa yin/_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_de dag shes pa'i lam ci 'dra/_/nga rgyal las ni ji lta bu/_/ji ltar bka' blo bde ba dang /_/thams cad dus 
[\[289a.4\]]su 'thun par 'gyur/_/byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_rtsod pa med pa shes pa'i las/_/nga rgyal las ni rtsod pa 'byung /_/nges par bka' blo bde ba ste/_/gus pa yis ni 'thun par 'gyur/_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_de dag lam ni ji lta 
[\[289a.5\]]bu/_/log pa'i lam yang ci 'dra zhig_/bla med byang chub lam du ni/_/ji ltar sems can rab tu 'god/_/byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_lam ni pha rol phyin pa drug_/theg pa chung ngu log pa'i lam/_/shes rab thabs la bslabs pa ni/_/sems 
[\[289a.6\]]can lam la 'dzud pa'o/_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_ji lta bur na nor chen 'gyur/_/de dag rnyed pa'ang ci 'dra zhig_/gter rnams kyang ni ji lta bu/_/gang gis de dag 'gro tshim byed/_/byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_nor bdun dang ni rtag tu ldan/_/
[\[289a.7\]]chos rnyed pa ni rnyed pa'o/_/de dag gter ni gzungs yin te/_rab tu rnyed nas tshim par byed/_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_de dag gi ni pha ma gang /_/nye du'i tshogs ni ji lta bu/_/g.yog rnams kyang ni ci 'dra zhig_/gang gis mkhas pa de dag mdzes/_/
[\[289b\]]
[\[289b.1\]]byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_ma ni shes rab pha ni thabs/_/nye du byang chub phyogs rnams yin/_/dge ba rnams ni g.yog yin te/_/des ni mkhas pa de dag mdzes/_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_ji ltar bdag med rtogs nas ni/_/byams pas 
[\[289b.2\]]'gro ba khyab par byed/_/bdag med pa dang byams pa dag_/de la ji ltar mtshungs par 'gyur/_/byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_gang dag stong nyid rab shes pa/_/de ni bdag med khong du chud/_/gang gis 'gro ba stong shes pa/_/de ni de yi byams 
[\[289b.3\]]pa mchog_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_'du byed rnams ni rang bzhin gyis/_/'ong ba med cing 'gro med na/_/las dang las kyi rnam par smin/_/ji lta bur na 'thad par 'gyur/_/byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_dam pa'i don du 'ong med de/_/gang 
[\[289b.4\]]du'ang 'gro ba yod ma yin/_/kun rdzob tu na lam 'jug cing /_/rnam par smin pa nye bar ston/_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_stong nyid mtshan ma med pa dang /_/smon lam rnam par spangs par ni/_/mtshan nyid gcig tu rab rtogs nas/_/de kho na ni ji ltar shes/_/byang 
[\[289b.5\]]chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_stong nyid gang yin de mtshan med/_/mtshan ma don du gnyer med pa/_/mtshan nyid gcig cing don gcig ste/_/rnam par thar pa'i sgo bstan to/_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_gang gis mya ngan 'das 'gro ba'i/_/
[\[289b.6\]]stong par rtog pa ji lta bu/_/sems can lta ba ji lta bu/_/yongs su smin byed ji lta bu/_/byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_shes rab kyis ni stong par rtogs/_/thabs kyis sems can rnams la lta/_/snying rjes yongs su smin par byed/_/des 
[\[289b.7\]]ni mya ngan 'das par 'gro/_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_chos 'di dag ni chos nyid kyis/_/skye ba med cing 'byung med par/_/tshul mchog 'di 'dra shes nas ni/_/gang gis 'khor bar skye bar 'gyur/_/byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_mi skye ba 
[\[290a\]]
[\[290a.1\]]dang mi 'byung ba/_/de ni shes rab spyod yul lo/_/bsams bzhin du ni gang skye ba/_/de ni thabs kyi tshul yin no/_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_gtong la dga' bar ji ltar 'gyur/_/ji lta bur ni phyir mi ldog_/ji ltar bzod pa phun sum tshogs/_/
[\[290a.2\]]ji ltar rnam par nges par 'gyur/_/byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_mnyam par gnas par gtong la dga'/_/chos kyi dbyings las phyir mi ldog_/mi skye ba la bzod pa ste/_/chos shes nas ni rnam par nges/_/sil snyan rnams kyis smras pa/_byang 
[\[290a.3\]]chub snying po snying po gang /_/rdzogs pa'i byang chub mtshan nyid gang /_/ci phyir rdzogs sangs rgyas shes bya/_/de bzhin gshegs pa ji ltar grol/_/byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs rnams kyis smras pa/_byang chub snying po nam mkha' ste/_/byang chub nam mkha'i mtshan nyid 
[\[290a.4\]]do/_/gang yang kun nas gti mug med/_/de bzhin nyid phyir de bzhin gshegs/_/de nas mi 'am ci'i rgyal po'i bu de dag gis tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag thos nas mi skye ba'i chos la bzod pa rab tu thob bo/_/de dag gis bzod pa rab tu thob nas rang rang gi gos dang rgyan bcom 
[\[290a.5\]]ldan 'das la mngon par bkab ste/_'di skad ces kyang gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das gang gi slad du bdag cag gis nyan pa'i rna ba 'di lta bu thob pas sangs rgyas ni bdag cag gi ched du byung ngo /_/bcom ldan 'das sil snyan dang glu dbyangs 'di dag las the tsom 'dri ba'i sgra 
[\[290a.6\]]'di lta bu byung ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs 'di dag gis dris pa de dag thams cad kyi lan btab pas 'khor 'di'i dogs pa thams cad spangs pa dang /_bdag cag kyang chos kyi snang ba dpag tu ma mchis pa skyes pa ngo mtshar to/_/bcom ldan 'das sil snyan de dag 
[\[290a.7\]]las sgra 'di lta bu byung ba'i rgyu ci lags/_su'i byin gyi rlabs kyis na byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs 'di dag gis lan 'debs par gyur/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_bzhin [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]dag de dag thams cad ni sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi brlabs su blta'o/_/bzhin 
[\[290b\]]
[\[290b.1\]{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]dag de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyi brlabs kyis/_nam mkha' las kyang chos kyi sgra 'di lta bu byung na sil snyan las chos kyi sgra ci phyir mi 'byung ste/_bzhin [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]dag de ltar de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyi rlabs ni bsam gyis mi khyab bo/_/mi 'am ci'i rgyal po'i 
[\[290b.2\]]bu de dag gis gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das sems can thams cad kyis sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs bsam gyis mi khyab pa thob par gyur cig/_//bam po bzhi pa/_de nas mi 'am ci'i rgyal po sdong po'i slas mi 'am ci mo brgyad khri bzhi stong mu tig kha 
[\[290b.3\]]dog sna tshogs kyi do shal thogs nas bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der dong ste phyin pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal nas ji ltar thogs pa'i mu tig gi do shal de dag gis bcom ldan 'das la mngon par bkab bo/_/mu tig gi do shal de dag phul ma thag tu 
[\[290b.4\]]sangs rgyas kyi mthus de dag bcom ldan 'das kyi dbu gtsug gi drang thad kyi steng gi bar snang la mu tig gi do shal gyi khang pa brtsegs pa brgyad khri bzhi stong du gyur te/_khang pa brtsegs pa de dag thams cad kyang gru bzhi lham pa/_ka ba bzhi zug pa/_cha mnyam pa/_ngos shin 
[\[290b.5\]]tu rnam par phye ba sha stag go/_/khang pa brtsegs pa de dag ni rin po ches spras pa'i khri rtsa ba yang rin po che las byas pa/_lha'i ras bcos ma'i zung du ma bkram pa dag byung bar gyur to/_/khri de dag thams cad la yang de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku skyes bu chen po'i mtshan sum cu rtsa 
[\[290b.6\]]gnyis kyis legs par brgyan pa dag bzhugs par kun tu snang ngo /_/de nas mi 'am ci mo brgyad khri bzhi stong gis bcom ldan 'das kyi cho 'phrul 'di lta bu 'di mthong nas/_tshim zhing mgu ste yid rab tu dga' nas dga' ba dang /_yid bde ba skyes te bsam pa thag pa nas bla 
[\[290b.7\]]na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed do/_/de dag byang chub tu sems bskyed nas dga' zhing mchog tu dga' ste dad pa thob nas/_thams cad kyis sgra skad dang dbyangs gcig tu tshul dang 'thun pa'i tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gis bcom ldan 'das la 
[\[291a\]]
[\[291a.1\]]mngon par bstod do/_/bla na med pa'i dgra bcom mchog gsung ba/_/don dgongs mtshungs pa ma mchis bsam mi khyab/_/don dang chos dang 'dul ba rab bslabs pa/_/sems can mchog la mgo bos phyag 'tshal lo/_/'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug tha ba 'jig_/
[\[291a.2\]]dgon par bzhugs dgyes bsnyengs pa mi mnga' ba/_/rin chen mdzod dang rin chen ston pa po/_/bcom ldan rgyal po'i rgyal la phyag 'tshal lo/_/spyan sngar dri bral nyon mongs mi mnga' ba/_/sa yi dbang po pha rol dgra 'joms pa/_/pad mo lta bur srid gsum mi gos pa/_/bde 
[\[291a.3\]]gshegs mchog brnyes khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/'gro ba'i nang na mig dang 'drar spyod cing /_/nyi zla 'di yi 'od ni spa bkong ba/_/'khor los sgyur ba'i rigs su rgyal por skyes/_/'khor lo mtshan mnga' khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/ming dang gzugs la nam yang mi chags shing /_/
[\[291a.4\]]mgon dang dpung dang skyabs dang dpung gnyen gyur/_/khyod dang mtshungs med lhag pa ga la zhig_/mtha' yas dbyangs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal lo/_/brnyes dang ma brnyes nam yang chags mi mnga'/_/'jig rten chos rnams zil gyis mnan te bzhugs/_/chags sred 
[\[291a.5\]]'khri shing dra ba grol mdzad pa/_/bcom ldan 'jig rten mgon la phyag 'tshal lo/_/dul ba thugs ni rtag tu mnyam par gzhag_/nyon mongs bsregs te stobs bcus rab bzhugs shing /_/'gro la zang zing mi mnga' sbyin stsol pa/_/sbyin pa'i gnas la mgo bos 
[\[291a.6\]]phyag 'tshal lo/_/smra mchog dam pa mtshan gyi 'od 'phros pa/_/lam log phyin ci log ni rnam par spangs/_/'jam zhing snum la snyan pa'i gsung gsung pa/_/srid pa gsum las 'phags la phyag 'tshal lo/_/mi mchog gdung bas gdungs pa mi mnga' ba/_/rdzu 
[\[291a.7\]]'phrul gyis ni bye ba'i zhing du gshegs/_/'gro ba rab tu sgrol ba'i gru mdzad pa/_/bcom ldan bran 'dra khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/bcom ldan brtson 'grus dag la ston mdzad pa/_/chags shing zhen pa'i 'gro dang mngon shes drug_/ldan zhing mkhas pa'i nyan thos 
[\[291b\]]
[\[291b.1\]]rnams kyang 'grel/_/'gro drug nye gshegs khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/gser gyi kha dog 'dra la phyag 'tshal lo/_/zhi gnas dbang po dul la phyag 'tshal lo/_/ston gyi zla 'dra'i zhal la phyag 'tshal lo/_/mi mchog yon tan mnga' la phyag 'tshal lo/_/nyon mongs 
[\[291b.2\]]bsregs shing bsnyengs pa mi mnga' ba/_/skye dgu 'di dag bsgral dka' chu bzhi sgrol/_/bsgoms shing dbang po thul ba'i bcom ldan 'das/_/khyod la skyabs su mchi bar mchid kyis 'tshal/_/thub pas sbyin pa bsam gyis mi khyab stsal/_/bslangs na dbang po 
[\[291b.3\]]dgyes par rab 'gyur ba/_/pha rol dag la sbyin pa mchog stsol ba/_/gtong dpa' bcom ldan khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/sems can nga rgyal rgyags khengs gti mug pa/_/rgyu dang rkyen dang bya ba 'gal bgyid rnams/_/'phags pa bla na med pa'i lam mchog la/_/rnam par 
[\[291b.4\]]'dren pa chen po khyod kyis bkod/_/phung po rang bzhin gyis ni stong mkhyen nas/_/las dang nyon mongs zil gyis mnan te bzhugs/_/'dren pa 'gro ba rnams kyi som nyi gcod/_/bcom ldan dus gsung khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/bden pa'i chos la dgyes shing mnyam

[\[297b.1\]]bo mi 'am ci'i slas brgyad khri bzhi stong po de dag gis bsam pa thag pa nas dge ba'i rtsa ba bskrun pa 'dis 'di ni shi 'phos nas bud med kyi dngos po las gyur te skyes pa'i lus thob nas dga' ldan gyi lha rnams dang skal ba mnyam par skye bar 'gyur ro/_/der skyes nas kyang byang chub

[\[301a.1\]]phug gi brag ca lta bu ste/_/de 'drar sgra ni rab tu dben shes pa/_/sgra rnams kun la 'dod pa'i chags pa med/_/bdag ni nam mkha'i ngos gnas mi ltung ste/_/mkha' las ltung ba'i gnas kyang yod ma yin/_/chos 'di nam mkha' 'dra bar bsgoms

[\[301a.3\]]ri bar kho ra khor yug ri/_/de bzhin ri dang bcas pa'i sa rnams kun/_/nam mkha'i ngos kyis phye bar rig nas de/_/rdzu 'phrul stobs kyis bye ba'i zhing du 'gro/_/sa dang chu dang me dang rlung dag dang /_/khams bzhi 'di dag nam mkha'i ngos mnyam par/_/dpa' bo tshul 
[\[301a.4\]]'di khong du chud pa dag_/mkha' las bye ba mtha' yas zhing du 'gro/_/srid pa gsum na sgra dang skad kyi bag_/bzang ngan de bzhin 'bring po ci yod pa/_/de yi mtha' ma bskal pa brgyar brjod kyang /_/sngon gyi rang bzhin mi snang rgyu ba med/_/rang byung ba ni mtshan mchog 
[\[301a.5\]]ma yin yang /_/sems kyi chos kyis gzugs kyis rab tu phye/_/sems dang yid ni mtshungs pa dben par gyur/_/rgyal ba thams cad chos mtshungs nyid phyir mnyam/_/sngon gyi srid par byang chub spyad spyod cing /_/dge ba brgya stong rab tu bsags nas ni/_/byang chub 
[\[301a.6\]]mnyam zhing mnyam par rab shes pa/_/grags pa chen po rnams ni lung bstan 'gyur/_/chos rnams kun ni rang yul yang dag ste/_/mtha' yas phyir ni rgyal ba'i yul du mnyam/_/mnyam nyid 'di dag gang gis rab shes pa/_/yon tan ldan pa de dag lung bstan 'gyur/_/

[\[301b\]]
[\[301b.1\]]min la/_/mtshungs med mi skye ba la bzod pa na/_/mi dbang 'gyur zhes rgyal bas lung bstan to/_/chos rnams kun dang bdag tu rang bzhin mnyam/_/bdag ni bdag med stong pa'i rang bzhin te/_/stong pa nam mkha' 'dra ba'i ngo bo nyid/_/byang chub rtag tu mkha' mnyam rang

[\[302b\]]
[\[302b.1\]]de'i tshe de'i dus na gling bzhi la dbang ba'i 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal pos 'dzin ces bya ba rin po che sna bdun dang ldan pa zhig byung ste/_de'i slas ni stong phrag bzhi bcu yod do/_/de'i bu ni stong tham pa yod de/_dpa' ba/_rtul phod pa/_yan lag mchog gi gzugs can

[\[302b.6\]]pas khyim nas khyim med par rab tu byung ngo /_/de na rgyal po'i bu de dag gis kyang rim gyis bu thu bo re re rgyal por bcug nas pha'i rjes su rab tu byung bar gyur to/_/rigs kyi bu de ltar na rnam grangs 'dis de bzhin gshegs pa de'i tshe'i tshad ring bar gyur to/_/rgyal po gzhon
